126/15/. dātu-kāmatā. saṃtiṣṭhate prāg evāḍhyānāṃ. matsariṇām
126/16/. api prāg eva tyāga-śīlānāṃ. yāni vā punas [tib. 68b]

126/18/. dharmā vistīrṇa-bhogatayā. (52b) teṣu kuleṣv āgatāgatān
126/19/. yācakān upasaṃharati. svayam eva gatvā dāneṣu puṇyeṣu
126/20/. kriyamāṇeṣu dakṣo 'n-alasa utthāna-saṃpannaḥ cittam

126/23/. karoti. evaṃ hi tad dānaṃ. yad upasthāyaka-vaiguṇyād

127/01/. dhyāśayas tu bodhisattvaḥ yathaivāpāya-samatikramaṃ

127/17/. cittam abhisaṃskṛtya dātavyam eva tad dharma-dānaṃ
127/18/. syāt. yady aham anena dharma-dānena mūka eva syāṃ

127/20/. eva syāt dharma-dānaṃ. prāg eva jñāna-saṃbhāra-vikalaḥ.
127/21/. sacet punaḥ pratyavekṣyamāno jānīyāt. nāsti me dharma-
127/22/. mātsarya-mala-samudācāraḥ. api tena bodhisattvenedaṃ
127/23/. pratisaṃśikṣitavyaṃ. aham ātmanaḥ kleśa-nirghātanārtham

128/01/. sāṃparāyikaṃ prabhūtataram an-anupradānāt paśyāmi. na

128/09/.  kathaṃ ca punar na dadāti. na khalu bodhisattvaḥ

129/01/. yam etat (53b) bhadra-mukha. na caitad yuṣmākam anu-

130/01/. dāsyāmi. punar api me āyatyāṃ dāna-vidveṣo bhaviṣyati.

132/01/.  ata ūrdhvam asmād eva sarva-dāna-prabhedāt tad-anyaḥ

152/16/. prajñā-saṃpadi niveśayati. evaṃ hi bodhisattvaḥ sarvā-
152/17/. kāreṇa sattvārtha-kriyā-śīlena samanvāgato bhavati. (62b)

153/01/. ya ity-evaṃ-rūpasya bodhisattvasya pūrvaṃ pādayor nipatyā=

227/24/. 'pi tāni dānādīni kuśala-mūlāni samudācarati. tatra caturbhir
227/25/. ākārair asaṃkliṣṭatā veditavyā. iha bodhisattvo nudita-
227/26/. cittaḥ tāṃ kuśalān dharmāṃ niṣevate na duḥkhī na durmanā
227/27/. avipratisārī bhavati tato-nidānaṃ. punar bodhisattvaḥ
^228
228/01/. param anapahatya dṛṣṭi-gatāny anabhiniviśya duścaritenā=
228/02/. vyāmiśrāṇi tāni kuśala-mūlāni dānādikāni samudācarati.
228/03/. punar bodhisattvaḥ satkṛtya sarvātmanā teṣv eva guṇa-
228/04/. darśī sāra-darśī śāṃta-darśī su-niścito 'para-pratyayo 'na=
228/05/. nya-neyaḥ tāṃ kuśalān dharmāṃ dānādīṃ samādāya
228/06/. vartate punar bodhisattvaḥ (90b) na teṣāṃ dānādīnāṃ
228/07/. kuśalānāṃ dharmāṇāṃ vipākaṃ pratikāṃkṣati cakravar=
228/08/. titvaṃ vā śakratvaṃ vā māratvaṃ vā brahmatvaṃ vā.
228/09/. nāpi parataḥ pratīkāraṃ pratyāśaṃsate. na tatra niśrito
228/10/. bhavati. [tib. 122a] na sarva-lābha-satkāra-ślokeṣu. nāpy
228/11/. aṃtataḥ kāya-jīvite 'pi niśrito bhavati. iti ya ebhir ākāraiḥ
228/12/. prasāda-prāmodya-sahagataś cāviṣamaś ca satkṛtya cānśritaś
228/13/. ca dānādīnāṃ samānārthatā-paryavasānānāṃ kuśalānāṃ
228/14/. dharmāṇāṃ samudācāraḥ. sā eṣāṃ asaṃkliṣṭatāty ucyate.
228/15/. viśuddhiḥ uttaptatā acalatā su-viśuddhatā ca veditavyā.
228/16/. tatrādhyāśaya-śuddhi-bhūmi-praviṣṭasya bodhisattvasya ut=
228/17/. taptāny acalāni caitāni kuśala-mūlāni bhavaṃti. tatreyam
228/18/. uttaptatā yad āśaya-śuddhasya bodhisattvasya sarve te kuśalā
228/19/. dharmā. a-pratisaṃkhyāna-karaṇīyā bhavaṃti. tatreyam
228/20/. acalanatā yad āśaya-śuddho bodhisattvo yathā pratilabdhe=
228/21/. bhyo yathopacitebhyaḥ kuśalebhyo dhermebhyo na parihīyate.
228/22/. na bhavyo bhavaty āyatyāṃ parihāṇāya nānyatra teṣāṃ
228/23/. rātriṃ-divānām atyayāt teṣām ātma-bhāvānāṃ samatikramāc
228/24/. candro vā śukla-pakṣe pratyupasthite vardhata eva kuśalair
228/25/. dharmair na parihīyate bodhisattvaḥ. niṣṭhā-gamanabhūmi-
228/26/. vyavasthitasya punar bodhisattvasyaikajāti-pratibaddhasya
^229
229/01/. caramabhavikasya vā ete kuśalā dharmāḥ su-viśuddhā
229/02/. veditavyā yeṣām uttari bodhisattva-bhūmau pariśuddhataratā
229/03/. nāsti. evaṃ tribhiḥ kāraṇair eṣāṃ kuśalānāṃ dharmāṇāṃ
229/04/. samudācāraḥ. dvābhyāṃ kāraṇābhyāṃ śreṣṭhatā. tribhiḥ
229/05/. kāraṇaiḥ su-viśuddhatā veditavyā dānādīnāṃ samānārthatā-
229/06/. 'vasānānāṃ.
229/07/.  tatra sarva-dānasya sarva-śīlasya vistareṇa yāvat sarva-
229/08/. samānārthatāyā āsevitāyāḥ su-viśodhitāyāḥ sakala-saṃpūrṇā=
229/09/. yāḥ [tib. 122b] anuttara-samyaksaṃbodhir vajra-sāra-
229/10/. śarīratā saddharma-cirasthitikatā ca phalam abhinirvartate.
229/11/. tatra duṣkara-dānena duṣkara-śīlena yavad duṣkara-samā=
229/12/. nārthatayā āsevitayā su-viśodhitayā tathāgatasyāpratisamā=
229/13/. ścarvādbhuta-dharma-samanvāgatatvaṃ phalam abhi(91a)=
229/14/. nirvartate. tatra sarvato-mukhena dānena sarvato-mukhena
229/15/. śīlena vistareṇa yavat sarvato-mukhayā samānārthatayā
229/16/. tathāgatasya sarvataḥ pradhāna-sattvair deva-manuṣyaiḥ 
229/17/. pūjyatvaṃ phalam abhinirvartate. tatra satpuruṣa-dānasya
229/18/. satpuruṣa-śīlasya yāvat satpuruṣa-samānārthatāyāḥ tatha=
229/19/. gatasya ye kecit sattvā apadā vā dvi-padā vā catuṣ-padā vā
229/20/. bahu-padā vā rūpiṇo vā arūpino vā saṃjñino vā asaṃjñino
229/21/. vā naivasaṃjñā-nāsaṃjñāyatanopagā vā. teṣāṃ sarveṣāṃ
229/22/. sattvānām agratvaṃ phalam abhinirvartate. tatra sarvā=
229/23/. kārasya dānasya sarvākārasya śīlasya vistarera yāvat
229/24/. sarvākārāyāḥ samānārthatāyās tathāgatasyāprameya-vicitra-
229/25/. puṇya-parigṛhītaṃ dvātriṃśan-mahāpuruṣa-lakṣaṇāśīty-anu=
229/26/. vyaṃjana-kāyatā phalam abhinirvartate. tatra vighātār=
^230
230/01/. thika-dānasya vighātārthika-śīlasya vistareṇa yāvad vighā=
230/02/. tārthika-samānārthatāyās tathāgatasya bodhimaṇḍa-niṣaṇ=
230/03/. ṇasya sarva-māra-pratyarthikāvighātāviheṭhāvikaṃpanāpra=
230/04/. tibalanatā phalam abhinirvartate. tatrehāmutra-sukhasya
230/05/. dānasyehāmutra-sukhasya śīlasya vistareṇa [tib. 123a]
230/06/. yāvad ihāmutra-sukhāyāḥ samānārthatāyās tathāgatasya
230/07/. para-dhyāna-vimokṣa-samādhi-samāpatti-sukhaṃ phalam
230/08/. abhinirvartate. tatra viśuddhasya dānasya viśuddhasya
230/09/. śīlasya vistareṇa yāvad viśuddhāyāḥ samānārthatāyāḥ
230/10/. āsevitāyāḥ su-viśodhitāyāḥ sakala-paripūrṇāyās tathā=
230/11/. gatasya sarvākāraś catasraḥ pariśuddhayaḥ āśraya-viśuddhir
230/12/. ālaṃbana-viśuddhiś citta-viśuddhir jñāna-viśuddhiḥ phalam
230/13/. abhinirvartate. tathā trīṇy ārakṣyāni daśa-bala-vaiśāradya-
230/14/. smṛty-upasthāna-sarvāveṇika-buddha-dharma-viśuddhiś ca
230/15/. phalam abhinirvartate. idam asya bodhisattvasya dānā=
230/16/. dīnāṃ kuśalānāṃ dharmāṇāṃ paryaṃta-gataṃ phalaṃ
230/17/. niruttaram. anyac cāsyāpramāṇam iṣṭam anavadyaṃ bo=
230/18/. dhisattva-caryāṣu saṃsarato veditavyaṃ.

230/21/.   saṃgraha-vastu-paṭalam (91b) paṃścadaśamaṃ.
^231
231/01/.  uddānaṃ.
231/02/.  ratna-pūjā mitra-sevā apramāṇaiś ca paścimaṃ.
231/03/.  tatra bodhisattvasya tathāgateṣu tathāgata-pūjā katamā.
231/04/. sā saṃasato daśa-vidhā veditavyā. śarīra-pūjā caitya-pūjā
231/05/. saṃmukha-pūjā vimukha-pūjā svayaṃkṛta-pūjā para-kārita-
231/06/. pūjā lābha-satkāra-pūjā udāra-pūjā asaṃkliṣṭa-pūjā prati=
231/07/. patti-pūjā ca.
231/08/.  [tib. 123b] tatra yad bodhisattvaḥ sākṣāt tathagata-
231/09/. rūpa-kāyam eva pūjayati. iyam asyocyate śarīra-pūjā.
231/10/.  tatra yad bodhisattvaḥ tathāgatam uddiśya stūpaṃ vā
231/11/. gahaṃ vā kūṭaṃ vā purāṇa-caityaṃ vā abhinava-caityaṃ
231/12/. vā pūjayati. iyam asyocyate caitya-pūjā.
231/13/.  yad bodhisattvaḥ tathāgata-kāyaṃ vā tathāgata-caityaṃ
231/14/. vā saṃmukhībhūtam adhyakṣaṃ pūjayati. iyaṃ asya
231/15/. saṃmukha-pūjety ucyate. 
231/16/.  tatra yad bodhisattvaḥ tathāgate vā tathāgata-caitye vā
231/17/. saṃmukha-pūjaṃ kurvann evaṃ adhyāśaya-sahagataṃ
231/18/. prasāda-sahagataṃ cittaṃ abhisaṃskaroti. yā ekasya tathā=
231/19/. gatasya dharmatā. sā sarveṣāṃ tathāgatānāṃ atītānāgata-
231/20/. pratyutpannānāṃ dharmatā. yā ekasya tathāgata-cāityasya
231/21/. dharmatā. sā sarveṣāṃ tathāgata-caityānāṃ dharmtā. ity
231/22/. ato 'ham etaṃ ca saṃmukhībhūtaṃ tathāgataṃ pūjayāmi
231/23/. sarvaṃś ca tān atītānagata-pratyāutpannāṃs tathāgatāṃ
231/24/. pūjayāmi. etac ca saṃmukhībhūtaṃ tathāgata-caityaṃ
231/25/. pūjayāmi. tad-anyāni ca daśaṣu dikṣv anaṃtāparyaṃteṣu
231/26/. loka-dhatuṣu sarvaṇi stūpāni gahāni kūṭāgārāṇi purāṇa-
231/27/. caityāny abhinava-caityāni pūjayāmi. itīyaṃ tāvad bodhi=
231/28/. sattvasya sādhāraṇā saṃmukhā. vimukhā ca tathāgata-pūjā
^232
232/01/. tathāgata-caitya-pūjā ca veditavyā. yat punar bodhisattvaḥ
232/02/. asaṃmukhībhūte tathāgate tathāgata-caitye vā tathāgata-
232/03/. cittam abhisaṃskṛtya pūjāṃ prayojayati sarva-buddhān
232/04/. uddiśya sarva-tathāgata-caityāni coddiśya. sā 'sya kevalā
232/05/. vimukhaiva [tib.124a] pūjā veditavyā. yad api bodhisattvaḥ
232/06/. parinirvṛte tathāgate tathāgatam uddiśya tathāgatasya śa=
232/07/. rīram (92a) stūpaṃ vā kārayati gahaṃ vā kūṭaṃ vā ekaṃ
232/08/. vā dvau vā saṃbahulāni vā yāvat koṭī-śata-sahasrāṇi yathā-
232/09/. śakti-yathā-balaṃ. iyam api bodhisattvasya tathāgateṣu
232/10/. vimukhā vipulā pūjā apramāṇa-puṇya-phalā 'neka-brāhma-
232/11/. puṇya-parigṛhītā. yathā bodhisattvaḥ anekair eva kalpair
232/12/. mahā-kalpair a-vinipāta-gāmī bhavati. na cānuttarāyāḥ
232/13/. samyaksaṃbodheḥ saṃbhāraṃ na paripūrayati tan-nidānaṃ.
232/14/. tatra yeyaṃ bodhisattvasya kevalaiva tathāgate tathāgata-
232/15/. caitye vā pūjā. iyam eva tāvad vipulā draṣṭavyā. tato
232/16/. vipulatara-puṇya-phalā kevalaiva vimukhā draṣṭavyā. tato
232/17/. vipulatama-puṇya-phalā sādhāraṇa-saṃmukha-vimukhā pū=
232/18/. jā draṣṭavyā.
232/19/.   tatra yad bodhisattvaḥ tathāgate vā tathāgata-caitye vā
232/20/. pūjāṃ kartu-kāmaḥ svayam eva svahastaṃ karoti. na
232/21/. dāsī-dāsa-karmakara-pauruṣeya-mitrāmātya-jñāti-sālohitaiḥ
232/22/. kārayaty ālasya-kausīdyaṃ pramāda-sthānaṃ vā niśritya.
232/23/. iyaṃ bodhisattvasya svayaṃkṛtā veditavyā. tatra yad bo=
232/24/. dhisattvaḥ tathāgate vā tathāgata-caitye vā pūjāṃ kartu-
232/25/. kāmaḥ na kevalaṃ svayam eva karoti api tu mātā-pitṛbhyāṃ
232/26/. karayati putra-dāreṇa dāsī-dāsa-[tib. 124b]karmakara-
^233
233/01/. pauruṣeyaiḥ mitrāmātya-jñāti-sālohitaiḥ paraiś ca rājabhiḥ
233/02/. rāja-mahāmātrair brāhmaṇair gṛhapatibhir naigama-jāna=
233/03/. padaiḥ dhanibhiḥ śreṣṭhibhiḥ sārthavāhair antataḥ strī-
233/04/. puruṣa-dāraka-dārikābhiḥ kṛpaṇair duḥkhitair ā-caṇḍālair
233/05/. api kārayati. tathā ācāryopādhyāyaiḥ sārdham-vihāry-aṃ=
233/06/. tevāsibhiḥ ca-brahmacāribhiś ca pravrajitair apy anya-
233/07/. tīrthyaiḥ tathāgate vā tathāgata-caitye vā pūjāṃ kārayati.
233/08/. iyaṃ bodhisattvasya sādhāraṇā pūjā sva-para-kṛtā veditavyā.
233/09/. yat punar bodhisattvaḥ parītte pūjā-karaṇīye deya-vastuni
233/10/. saṃvidyamāne karuṇā-sahagatena cetasā saṃciṃtya pareṣām
233/11/. eva vastv anuprayacchaty ete duḥkhitāḥ sattvāḥ alpa-pu=
233/12/. ṇyāś cāśaktāś ca tathāgate vā tathāgata-caitye vā kārāṃ
233/13/. kṛtva sukhitā bhavantv iti. pare ca tena vastunā (92b) ta=
233/14/. thāgate vā tathāgata-caitye vā pūjāṃ kurvaṃti. na bodhisat=
233/15/. tvaḥ. iyaṃ bodhisattvasya kevalā para-kṛtā pūjā veditavyā.
233/16/.  tatra yā kevalā svayaṃ-kṛtā. sā mahā-puṇya-phalā. yā
233/17/. kevalā para-kāritā. sā mahattama-puṇya-phalā. yā punaḥ
233/18/. sādhāraṇā. sā mahattama-puṇya-phalā niruttarā veditavyā.
233/19/.  tatra yad bodhisattvaḥ tathāgate vā tathāgata-caitye vā
233/20/. cīvara-piṇḍapāta-śayanāsana-[tib.125a]glāna-pratyaya-bhai=
233/21/. ṣajya-pariṣkārair abhivādana-vandana-pratyutthānāṃjali-
233/22/. karmabhiś ca dhūpa-gandhaiś cūrṇa-gandhair anulepana-
233/23/. gandhair vicitraiś ca mālyair vicitrair vādyair vicitraiś
233/24/. chattrair dhvaja-patākā-pradīpa-dānair vicitraiḥ stotrābhi=

233/26/. pūjāṃ karoti. tathā akṣayanikā-dānair maṇi-muktā-vai=
^234
234/01/. ḍūrya-śaṃkha-śīla-pravāḍāśmagarbha-musāragalva-jātarūpa-
234/02/. rajata-lohitikā-dakṣiṇāvarta-prabhṛtibhiḥ ratna-pradānaiḥ
234/03/. maṇi-kuṇḍala-keyūrādy-alaṃkāra-pradānair antataś ca gha=

234/05/. iyaṃ bodhisattvasya tathāgate vā tathāgata-caitye vā lābha-
234/06/. satkāra-pūjā veditavyā.
234/07/.  tatra yad bodhisattvaḥ dīrgha-kālikīṃ ca tathāgate vā

234/09/. prabhūta-vastukāṃ ca praṇīta-vastukāṃ ca saṃmukha-
234/10/. vimukhāṃ ca svayaṃ-kṛta-para-kṛtāṃ ca ghana-rasena ca

234/13/. dhau pariṇamayati. itīyaṃ bodhisattvasya saptākārodāra-

234/17/. pramāda-kausīdyād vā. satkṛtya karoti. nāpaviddhaṃ.
234/18/. [tib. 125b] avikṣiptaḥ karoty asaṃkliṣṭa-cittaḥ. na bud=
234/19/. dhābhiprasannānāṃ rājādīnām udāra-sattvānāṃ lābha-
234/20/. satkāra-hetoḥ kuhanārthaṃ pratirūpeṇa ca vastunā pūja=
234/21/. yati. na haritāla-lepana-ghṛta-snātra-guggulu-dhūpārka-

234/23/. sattvasya ṣaḍ-ākārā araṃkliṣṭā pūjā vcditavyā.
234/24/.  punar etām udārām asaṃkliṣṭāṃ lābha-satkāra-pūjñāṃ
234/25/. bodhisattvaḥ tathāgate vā tathāgata-caitye vā sva-bāhu-
^235
235/01/. balopārjitair vā bhogaiḥ karoti. parato vā pariyeṣitaiḥ.
235/02/. pariṣkāra-vaśitā-pratilabdhair vā . tatra pariṣkāra-vaśitā-
235/03/. prāpto bodhisattvaḥ dvau vā trīn vā saṃbahulān. vā sa=
235/04/. mucchrayān yāvat (93a) samucchraya-koṭi-nayuta-śata-
235/05/. sahasrāṇy anekāny abhinirmāya sarvais taiḥ samucchrayais
235/06/. tathāgateṣu praṇāmaṃ karoti. teṣāṃ samucchrayāṇām
235/07/. ekaikasya hasta-śataṃ hasta-sahasraṃ vā tato vā pareṇa
235/08/. nirmāya sarvais tair divya-samatikrāntaiḥ kusumaiḥ pareṇa
235/09/. sugandhibhiḥ parama-manoramais tāṃ tathāgatām abhya=
235/10/. vakirati. sarve ca te samucchrayā atyudārāṇi tathāgata-
235/11/. bhūta-guṇopasaṃhitāni stotrāṇi bhāṣante. sarvair eva ca
235/12/. taiḥ samucchrayaiḥ vicitrāṇy amātrāṇy agrāṇi praṇītāni
235/13/. keyūra-maṇi-kuṇḍalāni cchattra-dhvaja-patākāś ca tathāga=
235/14/. teṣūtsṛjaty āropayati. iyam evaṃbhāgīyā pariṣkāra-vaśitā-
235/15/. prāptasya bodhisattvasya sva-citta-pratibaddhā pūjā. na
235/16/. cāsya punar-buddhotpādaḥ [tib. 126a] pratyāśaṃsitavyaḥ
235/17/. prārthayitavyo bhavati. tat kasya hetoḥ. tathā hi tasyā=
235/18/. vaivartika-bhūmi-praviṣṭatvāt sarva-buddha-kṣetreṣv avya=
235/19/. hatā gatir bhavati. no cāpi bodhisattvasya sva-bāhu-balo-
235/20/. pārjitā bhogā bhavaṃti. nāpi ca parataḥ paryeṣita-labdhā
235/21/. vā. nāpi ca bodhisattvaḥ pariṣkāra-vaśitā-prāpto bhavati-
235/22/. api tu yā kācit tathāgata-pūjā jaṃbūdvīpe vā cāturdvīpe
235/23/. vā sāhasre vā dvi-sāhasre vā tri-sāhasra-mahāsāhasre vā
235/24/. yāvad daśasu dikṣv anantāparyanteṣu loka-dhātuṣu mṛdu-
235/25/. madhyādhimātrā pravartate. tāṃ sarvāṃ śrāddho bodhi=
235/26/. sattvaḥ prasāda-sahagatenodārādhimukti-sahagatena cetasā
^236
236/01/. spharitvā 'bhyanumodate. iyam api bodhisattvasyālpa-
236/02/. kṛcchreṇa mahatā apramāṇā tathāgata-pūjā bodhāya mahā-
236/03/. saṃbhāra-parigṛhītā. yasyāṃ bodhisattvena satata-samitaṃ
236/04/. kalyāṇa-cittena hṛṣṭa-cittena yogaḥ karaṇīyaḥ. tatra yad
236/05/. bodhisattvaḥ stoka-stokaṃ muhūrta-muhūrtam antato go-
236/06/. doha-mātram api ca sarva-prāṇi-bhūteṣu maitraṃ cittaṃ

254/01/. cirasthitikaḥ evam-āyuḥ paryaṃta iti vyavahāra-mātram

256/01/. ty amāyī vā na para-vaṃcana-śīlaḥ. hrīmāṃś ca bhavaty

257/01/. deśyamānam artha-pratisaraṇo bodhisattvaḥ satkṛtya śṛṇoti.

258/01/. sarvaiś ca punaś caturbhiḥ pratisaraṇaiḥ samyak-prayoga-

259/01/. dvidhā draṣṭavyaḥ. puṇya-saṃbhāro jñāna-saṃbhāraś ca.

260/01/. ca bodhipakṣyā dharmā veditavyāḥ. sa naiva kāyādīn dhar=

261/01/. nayānugatā ca vipaśyanā. ebhiś caturbhir ākārair bodhisat=

261/27/. vimocayati.

262/01/.  kathaṃ ca bodhisattvaḥ sattvanāṃ parīttāni kuśala-
262/02/. mūlāny apramāṇa-phalatāyām upanayati. iha bodhisattvaḥ

335/04/. vam evādhiśīla-vihāre te daśa śuddhāśayā manasikṛtā bha=
335/05/. vaṃti juṣṭāḥ partividdhāḥ. daśabhir aparair ākāraiḥ
335/06/. teṣāṃ cittāśaya-manasikārāṇām adhimātratvāt paripūrṇa=
335/07/. tvād adhiśīlavihāraṃ samatikramyādhicitta-vihāram anu=
335/08/. praviśati. śuddho me daśabhir ākāraiś cittāśaya iti ma=
335/09/. nasikāreṇa. abhavyaś cāhaṃ tasmād daśā(125b)kārāc
335/10/. chubhād āśayāt parihāṇāyeti manasikāreṇa. sarvāsrava-
335/11/. sāsraeṣu me dharmeṣu cittaṃ na praskandati pratikūla=
335/12/. tāṃ saṃtiṣṭhte iti manasikāreṇa. tat-paripakṣa-bhāva=
335/13/. nāyāṃ ca me vijñānaṃ saṃsthitam iti manasikāreṇa. abha=
335/14/. vyaś cāham asmāt pratipakṣāt punaḥ parihāṇāyeti ma=
335/15/. nasikāreṇa. abhavyaś cāham evaṃ dṛḍha-pratipakṣaḥ taiḥ
335/16/. sarvāsarva-sāsravair dharmaiḥ sarva-māraiś cābhibhavitum
335/17/. iti manasikāreṇa'saṃlīnaṃ ca me mānaraṃ pravartate
335/18/. sarva-buddha-dharmeṣu iti manasikāreṇa. sarva-duṣkara-
335/19/. caryāsu ca me nāsti vyatheti manasikāreṇa. adhimuktaṃ
335/20/. ca me mahāyāne cittam ekāṃtena na tad-anya[tib.174a]
335/21/. hīnayāneṣv iti manasikāreṇa. sarva-sattvārtha-kṛyābhira=
335/22/. taṃ ca me cittam iti manasikāreṇa. ebhir daśabhiś cittā=
335/23/. śaya-manasikāraiḥ praviśati.
335/24/.   adhicitta-vihāra-sthito bodhisattvaḥ sarva-saṃakārān ā=
335/25/. dīnavākārair vicitrair vidūṣayati. tebhyaś ca mānasam
335/26/. udvejyati. buddha-jñāne cānuśaṃsa-darśi bhavati vicitrair

336/01/. anuśaṃsākāraiḥ. tatra ca pṛhā-jāto bhavati ghana-rasena
336/02/. cchandena. sattva-dhātuṃ duḥkhitaṃ vyavalokayati citrair
336/03/. duḥkhākāraiḥ. teṣu ca sattveṣv apekṣā-citto bhavaty artha=
336/04/. pratisaraṇa-cittaḥ. sarva-saṃskāreṣv apramattaḥ. bodhā=
336/05/. yottapta-vīryaḥ. sattveṣu vipula-karuṇāśayḥ. teṣāṃ satt=
336/06/. vānām atyaṃta-duḥkha-vimokṣopāyaṃ sarva-kleśānāvara=
336/07/. ṇa-jñānam eva paśyati. tasya ca vimokṣasya samudāgamā=
336/08/. ya dharma-dhātau sarva-vikalpa-prapaṃcāra-saṃkleśopapatti-
336/09/. pratipakṣaṃ prajñāṃ paśyati. taṃ ca dhyāna-samādhi-
336/10/. samāpatti-nirhāraṃ bodhisattva-piṭaka-śravaṇa-pūrvakaṃ
336/11/. śravaṇa-nidānaṃ paśyati. dṛṣṭvā ca mahatā vīryāraṃbheṇa
336/12/. śruta-paryeṣṭim āpadyate. sad-dharma-śravaṇa-hetoḥ nāsti
336/13/. tad draviṇaṃ pariṣkāram ādhyātmika-bāhyaṃ vastu. yan
336/14/. na parityajati. nāsti sā guru-paricaryā. yāṃ nābhyupaga=
336/15/. cchati. nāsti ca kasyotpīḍā. yāṃ na abhyupagacchati sa prī=

336/21/. mucchraya-pratilaṃbhaiḥ. saced enaṃ kaścid evaṃ vadet.

351/01/. tva-kṣāṃtiṃ su-viśuddhāṃ labhate. sā punaḥ katamā.

411/01/.   tatrāyaṃ bodhisattva-bhūmāv anukramaḥ. gotra-stho
411/02/. bodhisattvo 'nuttarāyāṃ samyaksaṃbodhau cittam utpāda=
411/03/. yati. utpādita-cittaḥ sva-parārthe prayujyate. (150b)sva-
411/04/. parārthe prayujyamānaḥ a-saṃkleśopāyaṃ labhate. a-saṃ=
411/05/. kliṣṭaḥ a-khedopāyaṃ labhate. a-khinnaḥ kuśala-mūla-
411/06/. vṛddhy-upāyaṃ labhate. kuśala-mūlair vivardhamāno 'nu-
411/07/. ttarāṃ samyaksaṃbodhim abhisaṃbudhyate. sva-parārtha-
411/08/. prayoge ca a-saṃkleśopāye ca a-khedopāye ca kuśala-mūla-
411/09/. vṛddhy-upāye ca bodhi-prāptau ca pratipadyamāna ādita
411/10/. eva gaṃbhīrodāreṣu dharmeṣv adhimuktiṃ niveśayati.
411/11/. tad-adhimuktas tāṃ dharmāṃ paryeṣate. prayeṣya pare=
411/12/. ṣāṃ ca deśayati. svayaṃ ca pratipattyā saṃpādayati. saṃ=
411/13/. pādayaṃ yena ca pratipattavyaṃ yatra ca yad-arthaṃ ca.
411/14/. tena tatra tad-arthaṃ pratipadyate. tena tatra tad-arthaṃ
411/15/. pratipadyamāno yathā puṇyopacayo jñānopacayaś ca bhavati.
411/16/. tathā pratipadyate. puṇya-jñānopacitaḥ saṃsārānutsṛjano=
411/17/. pāye pratipadyate. tatra pratipadyamānaḥ saṃsārāsaṃkleśe
411/18/. pratipadyate. tatra pratipadyamānaḥ sva-sukhānadhyava=
411/19/. sāne pratipadyate. tatra pratipadyamānaḥ saṃsāra-duḥkhā
411/20/. parikhedaṃ pratipadyate. tenāparikhidyamānaḥ śāstrāṇy
411/21/. āgamayitvā sarva-śāstra-viśārado bhavati. śāstra-jño yasya
411/22/. yad yathā deśayitavyaṃ. taj jānaṃ loka-jño[tib. 212a]bha=
411/23/. vati. tathā śāstra-jño loka-jño yoniśo dharmāṃ parye=
411/24/. ṣate. tathaiṣamāṇaḥ sarva-sattva-sarva-saṃśaya-cchedana-
411/25/. samartho bhavati. tathā samarthaḥ para-saṃśaya-cchedana=
^412
412/01/. taḥ puṇyenābhivardharmānaḥ puṇya-saṃbhāraṃ paripūra=
412/02/. yatiṣvayaṃ ca jñānenābhivardhamāno jñāna-saṃbhāraṃ
412/03/. paripūrayati. paripūrayamānaḥ satyākārato bodhi-pakṣya-
412/04/. dharma-bhāvanāyāṃ prayujyate. bhāvanopāyaṃ ca prajā=
412/05/. nāti. tathā prayuktas tāṃ bhāvanāṃ mahāyāna-parinir=
412/06/. vāṇe pariṇamayati. na śrāvaka-pratyekabuddha-yāna-pari=
412/07/. nirvāṇe. tathopāya-kuśalaḥ sarva-bodhisattva-bhāṣi- - -
412/08/. - - - -bhāvanā-balena a-śruta-pūrvāś cāsya dharmāḥ sar=
412/09/. vākāraṃ pratibhāṃti. sa dhāraṇī pratibhāna-saṃpannaḥ
412/10/. tribhir vimokṣa-mukhaiḥ sarvāvaraṇa-prahāṇāya pratipad=
412/11/. yate. tatra  pratipadyamāna ātmahaḥ pareṣāṃ ca viprayā=
412/12/. sā(151a)bhimāna-prahāṇāya pratipanno bhavati. iyam asya
412/13/. sarvākārā pratipatti-saṃpat. sa tathā pratipannaḥ sarva-
412/14/. sattva-prativiśiṣṭo bhavaty api śrāvaka-pratyekabuddhebhyaḥ
412/15/. pratipatti-guṇa-viśeṣataś cānuśaṃsa-guṇa-viśeṣataś ca.
412/16/.   tatra pratipatti-guṇa-viśeṣaḥ. bodhisattvaḥ sva-para-
412/17/. hitāya pratipadyate parārtha-sva-kāryaś ca. śrāvakaḥ pra=
412/18/. tyekabuddhaś ca na tathā parārtha-svakāryaḥ. sarva-sat=
412/19/. tveṣv ātma-sama-citto bhavati. sama-cittaḥ satatam upa=
412/20/. kārāya pravartate. pratikāraṃ ca na pratikāṃkṣati. tathā
412/21/. prayuktaḥ[tib. 212b] satataṃ teṣāṃ hita-sukham āśaṃsate.
412/22/. tathā hita-sukhāśayaḥ avandhyaṃ teṣu prayogaṃ karoti.
412/23/. ity ayam uttarottarāhṛtaḥ pratipatti-guṇa-viśeṣaḥ.
^413
413/01/.   tatrānuśaṃsa-guṇa-viśeṣāḥ. bodhisattvo vyākaraṇaṃ
413/02/. labhate buddhebhyaḥ. na śrāvako na pratyekabuddhaḥ.
413/03/. vyākṛtaś cānratanīyāyāṃ bhūmāv avatiṣṭhate. tatra sthito
413/04/. 'vaśya-karaṇīye ca satata-karaṇīye cā-saṃmoṣa-dharmatāṃ
413/05/. pratilabhate. tathā a-saṃmoṣa-dharmatā buddha-bodhisat=
413/06/. tvaiḥ sarva-sattvāgra-prajñaptiṣu prajñapyate. sarva-kṛtyāni
413/07/. ca na parihāyati. a-prāpta-parihāṇyā ca na parihīyate.
413/08/. a-parihīyamāṇaḥ satata-samitaṃ kuśalair dharmaiḥ satataṃ
413/09/. vardhate. śukla-pakṣa iva tu vivardhamāno bhūto bo=
413/10/. dhisattvo bhavati. na pratirūpakaḥ. bhūto bodhisattvaḥ
413/11/. san sarvākāraṃ sarva-vinayopāyaṃ jānāti. jānan sarva-
413/12/. vyavasthāna-kuśalo bhavati yataś ca paryeṣyaṃ yac ca yena
413/13/. ca paryeṣyaṃ. paryeṣite ca tad etad vyavasthāpyate. vya=
413/14/. vasthāna-kuśalaḥ avavāda-kuśalo bhavati. avavāda-ku=
413/15/. śalaḥ apramāṇālaṃbanaṃ samādhi-rājaṃ pratilabhate.
413/16/. tat-samādhi-lābhī a-vandhya-dharma-deśako bhavati. ma=
413/17/. hāyāna-niryātaś ca bhavati. mahattvena tan-niryāto ma=
413/18/. hāyāna-saṃgrāhakaś ca bhavati. anyatamasāṃ mahāyāna-
413/19/. niryāṇāvasthāyām [tib. 213a]anyatamaṃ nāma lobhate.
413/20/. sarva-bodhisatta-sādhāraṇena ca nāmnā sarva-lokena bud=
^414
414/01/. dha-bodhisattvaiś ca apy upacaryate api stūyate. itīme
414/02/. anuśaṃsa-guṇa-viśeṣā veditavyāḥ. tasyaivaṃ-nāmnaḥ bo=
414/03/. dhisattva-liṃgāni veditavyāni [circa 28 syllables] pi pra=
414/04/. vrajita-pakṣe 'pi saṃpādayati ubhaya-pakṣe 'pi saṃpāda=
414/05/. yan pratipatti-sāraka ekāṃta-kuśala-pakṣādhyāśayo bha=
414/06/. vati. tathāśayo gṛhi-parigṛ[circa 21 syllables](151b) - -
414/07/. - - - - - - - - yāvat tiṣṭhate. tata ūrdhvaṃ saṃciṃtya
414/08/. bhavopapattiṃ gṛhṇāti. yatra buddha-bodhisattaiḥ samava=
414/09/. dhānaṃ sattvārthaḥ. tatra buddha-bodhisattvaiḥ satata-
414/10/. samitam avavādopastabdha-kuśala-pakṣa-viśeṣa-prāpty-avas=
414/11/. thām anubhavati. tad-anubhavānuśaṃsa-grāhī tad-viśeṣa-
414/12/. prāpty-avasthām ākrāmaty āvāsa-yogena. tad-avasthā-
414/13/. sthita-buddhir uttarottarāsu viśeṣāvasthāsu sarvākāraṃ
414/14/. parākramate ā niṣṭhā-gamanād a-saṃtuṣṭiḥ. tathā parā=
414/15/. kramaṇaḥ niṣṭhā-pratiṣṭhāṃ pratilabhate. yata uttari bhūyo
414/16/. na paryeṣate ānuttarya-prāptaḥ.

414/19/.        bodhisattva-bhūmer anukramaḥ samāptaḥ. samāptā
414/20/.        ca bodhisattva-bhūmiḥ.
